Word omissions for the 66 Sol windows

56 accepted · 171 rejected · 40 windows with at least one accepted omission

Existing proposals by gpt-6.1-sol from sol_scratch66_omission_word_bank_auto_v5.json. Re-reviewed independently by gpt-6-astra; no new proposals. Each preview removes one highlighted span in isolation. Character counts exclude punctuation. Combining edits requires a new read of the window.

Accepted without flags: 29. Accepted with flags: 27 (included in the accepted total).

Word level refers to one source lexical item, not one Chinese character. Necessary grammatical padding may be included.

Word omissions must remove a key person/group identity, a specific place, or a significant relationship. Standalone markers such as 忽然、就、不、因此 are excluded even when their deletion changes meaning. Minor attributes and quantity changes alone do not satisfy this additional key-content check.

Failed checks · key content removed (168) · valid omission unit (142) · information omitted (37) · fluent (1). One rejected candidate can fail more than one check.

Acceptance requires yes on all 6 checks — information omitted, fluent, deletion only, valid omission unit, valid in context, key content removed. A no or uncertain answer causes rejection. Fluency requires natural, grammatical Chinese.

The omission treatment removes source information through deletion. Fluent omissions can be accepted with additional semantic distortion flags. These flags record secondary mistranslation effects without causing rejection. Possible other meaning changed records uncertainty about the secondary effect.

Flags on accepted omissions · recoverable from context (22) · ambiguous reference (5) · other meaning changed (4) · possible other meaning changed (2). A candidate can have several flags.

Re-reviewed 227 unchanged proposals from sol_scratch66_omission_word_bank_auto_v5.json. No new proposals were requested. Prior decisions were not shown to the reviewer.

The deleted possessive is one relationship-bearing lexical item, with 的 as grammatical padding.

The later 他家 preserves the household as the party destroyed, but not its explicit identification as the party bearing the sin.

Removing 现在 drops explicit present-moment emphasis and the transition into the directive, not a distinct substantive fact. A standalone temporal/discourse marker is also excluded from the requested omission units.
  • Judges 18:14–17 · 年轻 — The edited verse no longer says that the Levite was young. Although this is a fluent deletion of one aligned lexical attribute, age is a minor description here: the Levite's identity, residence, and connection to Micah's house remain intact.
  • Judges 18:14–17 · 雕刻的 (recoverable from context) — 神像 no longer specifies that the image taken was carved. This is a fluent, aligned attribute deletion, but manufacturing type is not an eligible person, place, or relationship detail. The carving detail is recoverable from the matching inventory in 18:14.
  • Judges 18:22–23 · 这么 — Deleting 这么 is mechanically deletion-only and leaves natural Chinese. Here 'such'/这么 emphasizes the size of the company, while 一大群人 still states that Micah assembled a large group. No substantive person, place, or relationship detail disappears. This degree-emphasis deletion therefore fails both the information-loss and eligible-unit requirements.
  • Judges 18:27–28 · 平静而 — Deleting 平静而 removes the source description 'quiet' while retaining 'unsuspecting'; these are distinct descriptions. Removing the adjacent conjunction leaves fluent Chinese. Although this is one adjective with grammatical padding, it fails the bank’s eligibility rule because it removes an attribute rather than a person, place, or significant relationship.
  • Judges 18:27–28 · 而毫无戒备 — Deleting 而毫无戒备 removes 'unsuspecting' and leaves a fluent description of the people as quiet. Quietness does not preserve their lack of suspicion or preparedness. The deletion corresponds to one English adjective with an adjacent conjunction, but this attribute is outside the bank’s eligible key-content categories.
  • Judges 18:29–31 · 从前 (possible other meaning changed · recoverable from context) — Deleting 从前 removes the explicit qualification that Laish was the city's former name. The renaming narrative allows that chronology to be inferred. The edited 那城名叫拉亿 may instead suggest a concurrent name, potentially putting it in tension with 起名叫但. Although fluent and genuinely informative, this deletion targets an excluded temporal marker, not an eligible identity, place, or relationship.
  • Judges 18:29–31 · 整个 — The retained 一直都在那里 still says that the image remained there throughout the specified period. Removing 整个 reduces redundant emphasis on completeness without losing the substantive duration claim. It also targets a completeness modifier rather than an eligible key-content item.
  • Judges 9:1–2 · 请 — Deleting 请, aligned with 'Please,' makes the request less polite but preserves its substantive content. The resulting imperative is natural Chinese. This politeness marker is excluded from eligible word omissions and removes no key narrative fact.
  • Judges 9:1–2 · 所有的 — Deleting 所有的 removes the explicit requirement to ask all the leaders of Shechem; 示剑首领 identifies the leaders without expressly requiring exhaustive coverage. The edit is fluent and aligned with 'all,' but this quantifier-only omission fails the key-content eligibility rule.
  • Judges 9:1–2 · 七十个 — Deleting 七十个 removes the stated number of Jerubbaal's sons while leaving their identity, kinship, and proposed rule intact. The numeral is one lexical item with ordinary classifier padding, and the result is fluent, but an isolated quantity omission is ineligible.
  • Judges 9:9–10 · 却 — Deleting 却 removes the contrast signaled by English 'But', but preserves the speaker, reply, and substantive facts. The result is fluent and deletion-only. This discourse marker is excluded from eligible word omissions and removes no key actor, place, or relationship.
  • Judges 9:9–10 · 于是， — This standalone marker is excluded by the study's key-content rule, even if deleting it changes meaning. No model judgment can override this exclusion.
  • Judges 9:13–14 · 却 — Deleting 却 removes the contrastive framing aligned with 'But', but preserves the grapevine's reply and all its substantive content. The resulting Chinese is fluent and the edit is deletion-only. This is a discourse-marker deletion, not an eligible key-content lexical omission.
  • Judges 9:13–14 · 最后， — This standalone marker is excluded by the study's key-content rule, even if deleting it changes meaning. No model judgment can override this exclusion.
  • Judges 9:15–17 · 真心 — Deleting 真心 removes the sincerity qualification conveyed by 'really' in this context: the condition now concerns anointing alone, rather than sincere anointing. The exact deletion is fluent, but sincerity is an evaluative/manner detail, not an eligible identity, place, or significant relationship.
  • Judges 9:15–17 · 和正直 — Deleting 和正直 removes the separately stated honesty criterion while retaining faithfulness in 忠诚. The deletion is fluent and removes one lexical quality with its conjunction, but that evaluative quality does not meet the bank's key-content requirement.
  • Judges 9:15–17 · 生命 — 冒着危险 is fluent but states only unspecified danger, whereas 冒着生命危险 specifies that the father's life was at risk. This is a genuine lexical omission, but the nature or severity of the danger is not an eligible identity, place, or significant relationship.

The same people remain accountable for that killing, so no substantive bloodguilt information disappears. 都承担这罪 is grammatical. The deletion fails the unit requirement because it removes no eligible key identity, place, or relationship detail.
  • Judges 9:21–25 · 所有 — Deleting 所有 removes the explicit claim that every passerby was robbed. 抢劫从那条路经过的人 remains natural but does not require exhaustive coverage. This is a quantification-only omission, not the loss of a key group identity, place, or significant relationship, so it fails the bank’s unit and key-content requirements.
  • Judges 9:30–33 · 暗中 — Deleting 暗中 removes the source's explicit secrecy: Zebul still sends messengers to Abimelech, but no longer does so covertly. The exact result is fluent and deletion-only.   • Judges 9:30–33 · 在今晚 — Deleting 在今晚 removes the explicit instruction to come tonight. 现在 corresponds to 'Now then' and does not preserve that nighttime specification. The result is fluent and deletion-only, but this temporal deletion fails the unit and key-content requirements: it removes no qualifying actor, place, or significant relationship.

The Chinese remains fluent and the deletion is exact. However, this is an isolated quantificational change, not the loss of an eligible identity, place, or significant relationship.

After　耶何耶大吩咐统领军队的众百夫长说：“把她从队列之间带出去，凡跟随她的人，都用刀杀死。”因为祭司曾说：“不可在耶和华的殿里杀她。”

Deleting 祭司 removes the explicit identification of Jehoiada as a priest while leaving natural Chinese. Now Elisha was sitting in his house, and the elders were sitting with him. Look, when the messenger comes, shut the door to keep him out. Is not the sound of his master’s footsteps behind him?”

After　那时，以利沙正坐在自己家中，长老们也与他同坐。王派了一个使者先去，但使者还没到，以利沙就对长老们说：“你们看见了吗？这个凶手派人来砍头！你们留意，使者来的时候，就关上门，把他挡在外面。在他后面，不是有他主人的脚步声吗？”

Deleting 我的 removes the explicit identification of Elisha as the intended victim. 砍头 naturally means beheading without specifying whose head; it does not itself preserve 'my.' This is a fluent, deletion-only removal of one possessive lexical item, with 的 as grammatical padding.

于是，但人从他们的宗族中派出五个勇士，都是琐拉和以实陶人，去窥探、勘察那地。他们吩咐这五个人说：“你们去勘察那地。”这五个人进入以法莲山地，来到米迦的家，在那里过夜。

After　于是，但人从他们的宗族中派出五个勇士，都是琐拉和以实陶人，去窥探、勘察那地。他们吩咐这五个人说：“你们去勘察那地。”这五个人进入山地，来到米迦的家，在那里过夜。

Deleting the single place name 以法莲 leaves the fluent 进入山地 but removes the identity of the hill country the scouts entered. The locative action remains intact, and the displayed window does not otherwise identify that region.
